West of House
You are standing in an open field west of a white house, with a boarded front door.
There is a small mailbox here.


Starting location: West House
Target score: 100
Model: openai/gpt-4o-mini | Agent type: smart
Maximum steps: 500

LESSONS LEARNED FROM PREVIOUS EPISODES:
Repeat these positive behaviors:
1. 'open window' then 'west' from Behind House entered the Kitchen and recorded +10. Repeat this early surface route, and take the egg first if it is available.
2. Living Room: 'push rug', 'open trap', then 'down' recorded +25 once. Treat it as a one-time event, never a repeat goal.
3. Take a light source and run 'inventory' to confirm it before going down. The trap door shuts behind you.
4. Keep the lantern in hand at all times. Darkness was the direct precursor of both deaths.
5. Earlier 'take art' (+4) and 'put treasures in case' (+6) scored. Carry found valuables home and deposit them.
6. After about 10 scoreless moves, name one untried exit or object. 'examine' carried items before looping.
7. Have each state note name the true room and darkness status. Check it before choosing any movement verb.
8. Credit points only to recorded score events, and remember that death subtracts 10 from the final score.
Avoid these negative behaviors:
1. Never move in a pitch-black room. 'south' from the dark Cellar met a grue: -10, death, episode over.
2. Don't descend without a confirmed light source. This run reached the Cellar with nothing lit and had no safe action left.
3. Don't drop, stash, or leave the lantern. Dropping it in the Studio led to dark moves and death in a prior run.
4. Don't shuttle among the Cellar, East Chasm, Gallery, Studio and Troll room. Earlier runs spent about 350 moves there for zero points.
5. Don't adopt an 'up' or trap-door revisit goal. The +25 was a one-time 'down', and the goal text is stale.
6. Don't re-enter the Troll room. Past visits gave no points and the notes kept flagging it as blocked.
7. Don't probe dark side rooms like the Attic for no reason. The up-and-down detour cost two moves for nothing.
8. Don't burn turns on open, close, shake or put with the sack, garlic or lunch. None of it has scored.
Apply these lessons when relevant; current game feedback takes precedence.

Historian episode review — episode 25
================================================

Review source: llm
Lessons: 8 positive + 8 negative (configured per category: 8)

The Actor's best work was early and score-backed: 'take egg' (+5), 'west' into the Kitchen (+10), 'put egg in case' (+5) and 'down' (+25, one-time) are all recorded score events. Later, 'take art' (+4) and 'put treasures in case' (+6) were also recorded. That made 55 before death. The grue death cost 10, so the final score was 45.

Then it stalled: roughly 340 moves shuttling among Cellar, East Chasm, Gallery, Studio and Troll room scored nothing. The stale '+25 up' goal was reissued about 20 times. The Actor also picked up and dropped junk (pepper, garlic, lunch). Worst, it put down the lantern in the dark Cellar and moved south, then died. The truncated trajectory leaves unconfirmed whether the lantern was ever lit. Next time: lantern and egg first, check inventory and light before any 'down', never move in the dark, and try untried exits.

LESSONS LEARNED
================================
POSITIVES TO REPEAT
1. Take the egg early. 'take egg' (+5) and 'put egg in case' (+5) were recorded; get light and egg before the trap door.
2. 'open window' then 'west' from Behind House entered the Kitchen and recorded +10. Repeat this early surface route.
3. Before any 'down' or underground move, run 'inventory' and confirm the lantern is held and lit. Treat unlit descents as high risk.
4. Order goals: lantern and egg first, then trap door. The +25 'down' scored once and is not repeatable.
5. Carry found valuables home and deposit them: 'take art' (+4) and 'put treasures in case' (+6) were both recorded.
6. Once the lantern is lit, try one untried exit or object after about 10 scoreless moves, and note it.
7. Each state note should name the true room, darkness status and lantern status. Check them before choosing any movement verb.
8. If a room is dark, spend the turn on light or non-movement actions such as 'inventory' or 'examine'. Do not walk blind.

NEGATIVES TO AVOID
1. Never move in a dark room. 'south' from the dark Cellar met a grue: -10, death, episode over.
2. Don't put down the lantern, especially in the dark. It was dropped in the Cellar one move before the fatal step.
3. Don't adopt an 'up' or trap-door revisit goal. The +25 was a one-time 'down'; the stale goal was reissued about 20 times.
4. Don't shuttle among Cellar, East Chasm, Gallery and Studio. About 340 moves there recorded zero points; avoid 2-room loops.
5. Don't re-enter the Troll room. Repeated visits gave no points and the notes kept calling it blocked.
6. Don't spend turns taking or dropping pepper, garlic or lunch. None of it scored.
7. Don't make Attic or dark side-room detours without a reason. They cost moves and risk darkness.
8. Don't descend without a confirmed lantern, and don't trust a note claiming 'lantern in hand'. Verify with 'inventory'.
